## hard-limits

<a id="HL-01"></a>
### HL-01 — HL-01
> NO hardcoded prices in selection/emission paths (the grep-gate: golden levels ONLY in golden-gate.ts + diag scripts). ZONE-anchored, never price-anchored (R29).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-01.md`

<a id="HL-02"></a>
### HL-02 — HL-02
> NO fixed-constant SLs (R24/R25) — the SL is measured from the ladder geometry.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-02.md`

<a id="HL-03"></a>
### HL-03 — HL-03
> NO fallbacks-as-primary: a primary-path failure returns a LOUD named error; a 'fallback' producing a DIFFERENT artifact dressed as success is FALSE SUCCESS — banned.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-03.md`

<a id="HL-04"></a>
### HL-04 — HL-04
> NO identical-every-day output — the pipeline is day-native (R17), the band carries Mon→Fri.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-04.md`

<a id="HL-05"></a>
### HL-05 — HL-05
> NO fitted calibration to pass a gate — tuning a constant until green is the slop returning with a new number.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-05.md`

<a id="HL-06"></a>
### HL-06 — HL-06
> E3 NEVER overrides E1/E2 (R3).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-06.md`

<a id="HL-07"></a>
### HL-07 — HL-07
> THE FROZEN PAIR: sl-lmv.ts 912940832fac44bc... + trade-dedup.ts 33914c897e777540... byte-identical through EVERY change — a drift is a CRITICAL STOP for all desks.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-07.md`

<a id="HL-08"></a>
### HL-08 — HL-08
> Checkpoints/monday-gold-standard-achieved/ + v4.5.1_LIVE_MARKET_CAPABLE = LOCKED IMMUTABLE — never touched, never 'fixed'.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-08.md`

<a id="HL-09"></a>
### HL-09 — HL-09
> Doctrine QUOTED never synthesized (R19/D13).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-09.md`

<a id="HL-10"></a>
### HL-10 — HL-10
> NO caps on setups (D38) — the filter kills garbage, not count.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-10.md`

<a id="HL-11"></a>
### HL-11 — HL-11
> NO weekend holds (R11).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-11.md`

<a id="HL-12"></a>
### HL-12 — HL-12
> NO wall-clock in decision logic (the bible ban).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-12.md`

<a id="HL-13"></a>
### HL-13 — HL-13
> Subagents NEVER write canon docs (context_management/* is orchestrator-exclusive).
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-13.md`

<a id="HL-14"></a>
### HL-14 — HL-14
> NEVER the raw task tool as the primary dispatch channel; task_status is BANNED for liveness.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-14.md`

<a id="HL-15"></a>
### HL-15 — HL-15
> NEVER rebuild the canon pipeline — report-v452.ts is the source of truth; the V5 tree verifies; the generative-cascade gap is a DESIGNED build item, not a license to re-port.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-15.md`

<a id="HL-16"></a>
### HL-16 — HL-16
> The registry is append-only; the refusal tokens are exactly 8; the journal is sha256 — kernel law is not negotiable at the node level.
**Anchor:** `PLUTUS_MASTER_CONTEXT_INJECTOR.md §1.11`  ·  **Card:** `hard-limits/HL-16.md`

## terms

<a id="bom"></a>
### bom — BoM
> Break-of-Momentum: the zone born AT the break = the ORIGIN. Unmitigated + prior NONE/OPPOSITE → the 100% reaction zone, the highest-conviction anchor.

<a id="fortress"></a>
### fortress — FORTRESS
> 4+ strong holds (defense events) → the Fire→Earth transition → ~95% probability the next move FLIPS at that zone. The strongest single E1 signal.

<a id="mom"></a>
### mom — MoM
> Momentum: the zone born DURING the move. A VIRGIN MoM mid-BS/WL pathway = the DEATH ZONE (the one exception to the 3-mitigation rule).

<a id="obcv"></a>
### obcv — OBCV
> Order-Block Confirmation Vision gates: Displacement (body ≥1.5× avg AND range ≥1.5× ATR) · Freshness (bw ≥3.0 AND bf ≥0.65) · Temporal (a 30m orphan inside the OB → REJECT).

<a id="phantom-bom"></a>
### phantom_bom — PHANTOM_BOM
> Type-2 virgin MoM: large-OB + fakeout + pre-formation opposing-BoM reaction + macro-origin continuation (all-4 predicate). Classified at the formation window; the FORTRESS exemption to the death rule.
